{\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeyfJgESQS7E6hBF8drEp","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01F4baCVUL1tXjuaHtBSWnm2","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/qtclustering_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_mpi_r4/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","content":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - MPI Distributed Memory Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//   The expensive part of each outer iteration is the evaluation of every\n//   still-unclustered point as a candidate seed; those evaluations are\n//   completely independent of each other.  The seeds of one iteration are\n//   distributed round-robin over the MPI ranks, the globally best candidate\n//   cluster is selected with a single MPI_Allreduce(MPI_MAXLOC) and its\n//   member list is broadcast so that every rank keeps an identical view of\n//   the clustering state.\n//\n// Serial optimizations (semantics preserving):\n//   * the maximum distance of a candidate to the current cluster is updated\n//     incrementally instead of being recomputed from scratch,\n//   * candidates that can never be added again (max distance >= threshold is\n//     monotonically non-decreasing) are compacted away,\n//   * a uniform grid with cell size == threshold restricts the initial\n//     candidate set to the neighbourhood of the seed, since every cluster\n//     member must be within the threshold of the seed point,\n//   * squared distances are used throughout (monotone transformation of the\n//     Euclidean distance, so all comparisons are equivalent).\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Uniform bucket grid over the still unclustered points.  The cell size equals\n// the clustering threshold, hence all points that may end up in a cluster with\n// a given seed live in the 3x3 cell block around the seed's cell.\nstruct Grid {\n    int nx = 0, ny = 0;\n    double inv_cell = 0.0;\n    std::vector<int> start;    // CSR offsets, size nx*ny+1\n    std::vector<int> idx;      // point ids, ascending within each cell\n    std::vector<double> x, y;  // coordinates in grid order\n\n    inline int cellX(const double px) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(px * inv_cell);\n        if (c < 0) c = 0;\n        if (c >= nx) c = nx - 1;\n        return c;\n    }\n    inline int cellY(const double py) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(py * inv_cell);\n        if (c < 0) c = 0;\n        if (c >= ny) c = ny - 1;\n        return c;\n    }\n};\n\n// (Re-)build the grid from the given (ascending) list of unclustered points\nstatic void buildGrid(Grid& g, const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                      const std::vector<int>& active, const double threshold) {\n    const double cell = threshold;\n    g.inv_cell = 1.0 / cell;\n    g.nx = static_cast<int>(MAX_WIDTH / cell) + 1;\n    g.ny = static_cast<int>(MAX_HEIGHT / cell) + 1;\n\n    const int ncells = g.nx * g.ny;\n    g.start.assign(ncells + 1, 0);\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(active.size());\n    g.idx.resize(n);\n    g.x.resize(n);\n    g.y.resize(n);\n\n    // Counting sort of the active points into their cells\n    std::vector<int> cellOf(n);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const Point& p = points[active[i]];\n        const int c = g.cellY(p.y) * g.nx + g.cellX(p.x);\n        cellOf[i] = c;\n        ++g.start[c + 1];\n    }\n    for (int c = 0; c < ncells; ++c) {\n        g.start[c + 1] += g.start[c];\n    }\n    std::vector<int> cursor(g.start.begin(), g.start.end() - 1);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const int pos = cursor[cellOf[i]]++;\n        const int id = active[i];\n        g.idx[pos] = id;\n        g.x[pos] = points[id].x;\n        g.y[pos] = points[id].y;\n    }\n}\n\n// Scratch buffers for the candidate evaluation (reused across seeds)\nstruct Workspace {\n    std::vector<int> idx;\n    std::vector<double> x, y, d2;\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    std::vector<int> members;\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster; the members are stored in\n// ws.members (ordered exactly like in the sequential reference version).\nstatic int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                                    const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                    const Grid& grid,\n                                    const double threshold,\n                                    Workspace& ws) {\n    const double thr2 = threshold * threshold;\n    const Point sp = points[seed_point];\n\n    ws.members.clear();\n    ws.members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    // Collect all candidates within the threshold of the seed\n    ws.order.clear();\n    const int cx = grid.cellX(sp.x);\n    const int cy = grid.cellY(sp.y);\n    const int x0 = std::max(cx - 1, 0), x1 = std::min(cx + 1, grid.nx - 1);\n    const int y0 = std::max(cy - 1, 0), y1 = std::min(cy + 1, grid.ny - 1);\n    for (int gy = y0; gy <= y1; ++gy) {\n        const int row = gy * grid.nx;\n        for (int gx = x0; gx <= x1; ++gx) {\n            const int b = grid.start[row + gx], e = grid.start[row + gx + 1];\n            for (int p = b; p < e; ++p) {\n                if (grid.idx[p] == seed_point) continue;\n                const double dx = grid.x[p] - sp.x;\n                const double dy = grid.y[p] - sp.y;\n                const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n                if (d2 < thr2) ws.order.push_back(p);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // The reference implementation scans candidates in ascending point id and\n    // keeps the first of several equally good ones; reproduce that ordering.\n    std::sort(ws.order.begin(), ws.order.end(),\n              [&grid](int a, int b) { return grid.idx[a] < grid.idx[b]; });\n\n    int n = static_cast<int>(ws.order.size());\n    ws.idx.resize(n);\n    ws.x.resize(n);\n    ws.y.resize(n);\n    ws.d2.resize(n);\n    int best = -1;\n    double best_d2 = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const int p = ws.order[i];\n        ws.idx[i] = grid.idx[p];\n        ws.x[i] = grid.x[p];\n        ws.y[i] = grid.y[p];\n        const double dx = grid.x[p] - sp.x;\n        const double dy = grid.y[p] - sp.y;\n        const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n        ws.d2[i] = d2;\n        if (d2 < best_d2) {\n            best_d2 = d2;\n            best = i;\n        }\n    }\n\n    int* __restrict cidx = ws.idx.data();\n    double* __restrict cx_ = ws.x.data();\n    double* __restrict cy_ = ws.y.data();\n    double* __restrict cd2 = ws.d2.data();\n\n    // Iteratively add the candidate with the smallest cluster diameter\n    while (best >= 0) {\n        const int newMember = cidx[best];\n        const double nx = cx_[best], ny = cy_[best];\n        ws.members.push_back(newMember);\n\n        // Update the per-candidate maximum distance to the cluster, drop the\n        // candidates that exceeded the threshold and pick the next best one.\n        int w = 0;\n        int nextBest = -1;\n        double nextBestD2 = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            if (i == best) continue;\n            const double dx = cx_[i] - nx;\n            const double dy = cy_[i] - ny;\n            const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            const double m = d2 > cd2[i] ? d2 : cd2[i];\n            if (m < thr2) {\n                cidx[w] = cidx[i];\n                cx_[w] = cx_[i];\n                cy_[w] = cy_[i];\n                cd2[w] = m;\n                if (m < nextBestD2) {\n                    nextBestD2 = m;\n                    nextBest = w;\n                }\n                ++w;\n            }\n        }\n        n = w;\n        best = nextBest;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(ws.members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (distributed over MPI ranks)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    Grid grid;\n    Workspace ws;\n    std::vector<int> best_members;\n    best_members.reserve(N);\n    std::vector<int> recv_members(N);\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        buildGrid(grid, points, unclustered_indices, threshold);\n\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        best_members.clear();\n\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed; seeds are spread round-robin\n        // over the ranks, which balances the very different per-seed costs.\n        const int nseeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n        for (int i = rank; i < nseeds; i += nranks) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            const int cardinality =\n                generateCandidateCluster(seed, points, grid, threshold, ws);\n\n            // Better cardinality, or equal cardinality with a smaller seed id\n            // (the reference scans seeds in ascending order and keeps the first)\n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality ||\n                (cardinality == max_cardinality && seed < best_seed)) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_members = ws.members;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Global reduction: maximize cardinality, break ties by smallest seed.\n        // The key packs both criteria so that a single MAXLOC also yields the\n        // rank owning the winning cluster.\n        struct { long key; int rank; } in, out;\n        in.key = (max_cardinality >= 0)\n                     ? static_cast<long>(max_cardinality) * (N + 1L) + (N - best_seed)\n                     : -1L;\n        in.rank = rank;\n        MPI_Allreduce(&in, &out, 1, MPI_LONG_INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (out.key < 0) break; // No more clusters can be formed\n\n        const int winner = out.rank;\n        const int global_card = static_cast<int>(out.key / (N + 1L));\n        const int global_seed = N - static_cast<int>(out.key % (N + 1L));\n        if (global_card <= 0) break;\n\n        int count = global_card;\n        const int* src;\n        if (rank == winner) {\n            src = best_members.data();\n            MPI_Bcast(const_cast<int*>(src), count, MPI_INT, winner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        } else {\n            MPI_Bcast(recv_members.data(), count, MPI_INT, winner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n            src = recv_members.data();\n        }\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = global_seed;\n        cluster.members.assign(src, src + count);\n        for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {\n            clustered[src[i]] = 1;\n        }\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n        unclustered_indices.erase(\n            std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                          [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n            unclustered_indices.end()\n        );\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\",\n                   num_points, threshold);\n        }\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n        printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Generate synthetic data (deterministic, replicated on every rank)\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n\n    // Perform QT clustering\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n\n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n\n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n\n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 :\n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n        printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n        printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\",\n               total_clustered, num_points,\n               100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n        printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n        printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n\n        // Performance metrics\n        const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n        const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n        const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\",\n               clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults && rank == 0) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    int status = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);\n\n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                status = 1;\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n The seeds of one iteration are\n//   distributed round-robin over the MPI ranks, the globally best candidate\n//   cluster is selected with a single MPI_Allreduce(MPI_MAXLOC) and its\n//   member list is broadcast so that every rank keeps an identical view of\n//   the clustering state.\n//\n// Serial optimizations (semantics preserving):\n//   * the maximum distance of a candidate to the current cluster is updated\n//     incrementally instead of being recomputed from scratch,\n//   * candidates that can never be added again (max distance >= threshold is\n//     monotonically non-decreasing) are compacted away,\n//   * a uniform grid with cell size == threshold restricts the initial\n//     candidate set to the neighbourhood of the seed, since every cluster\n//     member must be within the threshold of the seed point,\n//   * squared distances are used throughout (monotone transformation of the\n//     Euclidean distance, so all comparisons are equivalent).\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Uniform bucket grid over the still unclustered points.  The cell size equals\n// the clustering threshold, hence all points that may end up in a cluster with\n// a given seed live in the 3x3 cell block around the seed's cell.\nstruct Grid {\n    int nx = 0, ny = 0;\n    double inv_cell = 0.0;\n    std::vector<int> start;    // CSR offsets, size nx*ny+1\n    std::vector<int> idx;      // point ids, ascending within each cell\n    std::vector<double> x, y;  // coordinates in grid order\n\n    inline int cellX(const double px) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(px * inv_cell);\n        if (c < 0) c = 0;\n        if (c >= nx) c = nx - 1;\n        return c;\n    }\n    inline int cellY(const double py) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(py * inv_cell);\n        if (c < 0) c = 0;\n        if (c >= ny) c = ny - 1;\n        return c;\n    }\n};\n\n// (Re-)build the grid from the given (ascending) list of unclustered points\nstatic void buildGrid(Grid& g, const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                      const std::vector<int>& active, const double threshold) {\n    const double cell = threshold;\n    g.inv_cell = 1.0 / cell;\n    g.nx = static_cast<int>(MAX_WIDTH / cell) + 1;\n    g.ny = static_cast<int>(MAX_HEIGHT / cell) + 1;\n\n    const int ncells = g.nx * g.ny;\n    g.start.assign(ncells + 1, 0);\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(active.size());\n    g.idx.resize(n);\n    g.x.resize(n);\n    g.y.resize(n);\n\n    // Counting sort of the active points into their cells\n    std::vector<int> cellOf(n);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const Point& p = points[active[i]];\n        const int c = g.cellY(p.y) * g.nx + g.cellX(p.x);\n        cellOf[i] = c;\n        ++g.start[c + 1];\n    }\n    for (int c = 0; c < ncells; ++c) {\n        g.start[c + 1] += g.start[c];\n    }\n    std::vector<int> cursor(g.start.begin(), g.start.end() - 1);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const int pos = cursor[cellOf[i]]++;\n        const int id = active[i];\n        g.idx[pos] = id;\n        g.x[pos] = points[id].x;\n        g.y[pos] = points[id].y;\n    }\n}\n\n// Scratch buffers for the candidate evaluation (reused across seeds)\nstruct Workspace {\n    std::vector<int> idx;\n    std::vector<double> x, y, d2;\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    std::vector<int> members;\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster; the members are stored in\n// ws.members (ordered exactly like in the sequential reference version).\nstatic int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                                    const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                    const Grid& grid,\n                                    const double threshold,\n                                    Workspace& ws) {\n    const double thr2 = threshold * threshold;\n    const Point sp = points[seed_point];\n\n    ws.members.clear();\n    ws.members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    // Collect all candidates within the threshold of the seed\n    ws.order.clear();\n    const int cx = grid.cellX(sp.x);\n    const int cy = grid.cellY(sp.y);\n    const int x0 = std::max(cx - 1, 0), x1 = std::min(cx + 1, grid.nx - 1);\n    const int y0 = std::max(cy - 1, 0), y1 = std::min(cy + 1, grid.ny - 1);\n    for (int gy = y0; gy <= y1; ++gy) {\n        const int row = gy * grid.nx;\n        for (int gx = x0; gx <= x1; ++gx) {\n            const int b = grid.start[row + gx], e = grid.start[row + gx + 1];\n            for (int p = b; p < e; ++p) {\n                if (grid.idx[p] == seed_point) continue;\n                const double dx = grid.x[p] - sp.x;\n                const double dy = grid.y[p] - sp.y;\n                const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n                if (d2 < thr2) ws.order.push_back(p);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // The reference implementation scans candidates in ascending point id and\n    // keeps the first of several equally good ones; reproduce that ordering.\n    std::sort(ws.order.begin(), ws.order.end(),\n              [&grid](int a, int b) { return grid.idx[a] < grid.idx[b]; });\n\n    int n = static_cast<int>(ws.order.size());\n    ws.idx.resize(n);\n    ws.x.resize(n);\n    ws.y.resize(n);\n    ws.d2.resize(n);\n    int best = -1;\n    double best_d2 = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const int p = ws.order[i];\n        ws.idx[i] = grid.idx[p];\n        ws.x[i] = grid.x[p];\n        ws.y[i] = grid.y[p];\n        const double dx = grid.x[p] - sp.x;\n        const double dy = grid.y[p] - sp.y;\n        const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n        ws.d2[i] = d2;\n        if (d2 < best_d2) {\n            best_d2 = d2;\n            best = i;\n        }\n    }\n\n    int* __restrict cidx = ws.idx.data();\n    double* __restrict cx_ = ws.x.data();\n    double* __restrict cy_ = ws.y.data();\n    double* __restrict cd2 = ws.d2.data();\n\n    // Iteratively add the candidate with the smallest cluster diameter\n    while (best >= 0) {\n        const int newMember = cidx[best];\n        const double nx = cx_[best], ny = cy_[best];\n        ws.members.push_back(newMember);\n\n        // Update the per-candidate maximum distance to the cluster, drop the\n        // candidates that exceeded the threshold and pick the next best one.\n        int w = 0;\n        int nextBest = -1;\n        double nextBestD2 = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            if (i == best) continue;\n            const double dx = cx_[i] - nx;\n            const double dy = cy_[i] - ny;\n            const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            const double m = d2 > cd2[i] ? d2 : cd2[i];\n            if (m < thr2) {\n                cidx[w] = cidx[i];\n                cx_[w] = cx_[i];\n                cy_[w] = cy_[i];\n                cd2[w] = m;\n                if (m < nextBestD2) {\n                    nextBestD2 = m;\n                    nextBest = w;\n                }\n                ++w;\n            }\n        }\n        n = w;\n        best = nextBest;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(ws.members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (distributed over MPI ranks)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    Grid grid;\n    Workspace ws;\n    std::vector<int> best_members;\n    best_members.reserve(N);\n    std::vector<int> recv_members(N);\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        buildGrid(grid, points, unclustered_indices, threshold);\n\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        best_members.clear();\n\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed; seeds are spread round-robin\n        // over the ranks, which balances the very different per-seed costs.\n        const int nseeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n        for (int i = rank; i < nseeds; i += nranks) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            const int cardinality =\n                generateCandidateCluster(seed, points, grid, threshold, ws);\n\n            // Better cardinality, or equal cardinality with a smaller seed id\n            // (the reference scans seeds in ascending order and keeps the first)\n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality ||\n                (cardinality == max_cardinality && seed < best_seed)) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_members = ws.members;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Global reduction: maximize cardinality, break ties by smallest seed.\n        // The key packs both criteria so that a single MAXLOC also yields the\n        // rank owning the winning cluster.\n        struct { long key; int rank; } in, out;\n        in.key = (max_cardinality >= 0)\n                     ? static_cast<long>(max_cardinality) * (N + 1L) + (N - best_seed)\n                     : -1L;\n        in.rank = rank;\n        MPI_Allreduce(&in, &out, 1, MPI_LONG_INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (out.key < 0) break; // No more clusters can be formed\n\n        const int winner = out.rank;\n        const int global_card = static_cast<int>(out.key / (N + 1L));\n        const int global_seed = N - static_cast<int>(out.key % (N + 1L));\n        if (global_card <= 0) break;\n\n        int count = global_card;\n        const int* src;\n        if (rank == winner) {\n            src = best_members.data();\n            MPI_Bcast(const_cast<int*>(src), count, MPI_INT, winner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        } else {\n            MPI_Bcast(recv_members.data(), count, MPI_INT, winner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n            src = recv_members.data();\n        }\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = global_seed;\n        cluster.members.assign(src, src + count);\n        for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {\n            clustered[src[i]] = 1;\n        }\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n        unclustered_indices.erase(\n            std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                          [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n            unclustered_indices.end()\n        );\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\",\n                   num_points, threshold);\n        }\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    if (rank  generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n\n    // Perform QT clustering\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    const std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n\n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n\n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 :\n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n        printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n        printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\",\n               total_clustered, num_points,\n               100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n        printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n        printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n\n        // Performance metrics\n        const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n        const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n        const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\",\n               clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults && rank == 0) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    int status = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                status = 1;\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return status;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":8,"newStart":1,"newLines":28,"lines":["-// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version","-// ","+// QT Clustering {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default:         validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 The seeds of one iteration are\n//   distributed round-robin over the MPI ranks, the globally best candidate\n//   cluster is selected with a single MPI_Allreduce(MPI_MAXLOC) and its\n//   member list is broadcast so that every rank keeps an identical view of\n//   the clustering state.\n//\n// Serial optimizations (semantics preserving):\n//   * the maximum distance of a candidate to the current cluster is updated\n//     incrementally instead of being recomputed from scratch,\n//   * candidates that can never be added again (max distance >= threshold is\n//     monotonically non-decreasing) are compacted away,\n//   * a uniform grid with cell size == threshold restricts the initial\n//     candidate set to the neighbourhood of the seed, since every cluster\n//     member must be within the threshold of the seed point,\n//   * squared distances are used throughout (monotone transformation of the\n//     Euclidean distance, so all comparisons are equivalent).\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Uniform bucket grid over the still unclustered points.  The cell size equals\n// the clustering threshold, hence all points that may end up in a cluster with\n// a given seed live in the 3x3 cell block around the seed's cell.\nstruct Grid {\n    int nx = 0, ny = 0;\n    double inv_cell = 0.0;\n    std::vector<int> start;    // CSR offsets, size nx*ny+1\n    std::vector<int> idx;      // point ids, ascending within each cell\n    std::vector<double> x, y;  // coordinates in grid order\n\n    inline int cellX(const double px) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(px * inv_cell);\n        if (c < 0) c = 0;\n        if (c >= nx) c = nx - 1;\n        return c;\n    }\n    inline int cellY(const double py) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(py * inv_cell);\n        if (c < 0) c = 0;\n        if (c >= ny) c = ny - 1;\n        return c;\n    }\n};\n\n// (Re-)build the grid from the given (ascending) list of unclustered points\nstatic void buildGrid(Grid& g, const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                      const std::vector<int>& active, const double threshold) {\n    const double cell = threshold;\n    g.inv_cell = 1.0 / cell;\n    g.nx = static_cast<int>(MAX_WIDTH / cell) + 1;\n    g.ny = static_cast<int>(MAX_HEIGHT / cell) + 1;\n\n    const int ncells = g.nx * g.ny;\n    g.start.assign(ncells + 1, 0);\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(active.size());\n    g.idx.resize(n);\n    g.x.resize(n);\n    g.y.resize(n);\n\n    // Counting sort of the active points into their cells\n    std::vector<int> cellOf(n);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const Point& p = points[active[i]];\n        const int c = g.cellY(p.y) * g.nx + g.cellX(p.x);\n        cellOf[i] = c;\n        ++g.start[c + 1];\n    }\n    for (int c = 0; c < ncells; ++c) {\n        g.start[c + 1] += g.start[c];\n    }\n    std::vector<int> cursor(g.start.begin(), g.start.end() - 1);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const int pos = cursor[cellOf[i]]++;\n        const int id = active[i];\n        g.idx[pos] = id;\n        g.x[pos] = points[id].x;\n        g.y[pos] = points[id].y;\n    }\n}\n\n// Scratch buffers for the candidate evaluation (reused across seeds)\nstruct Workspace {\n    std::vector<int> idx;\n    std::vector<double> x, y, d2;\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    std::vector<int> members;\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster; the members are stored in\n// ws.members (ordered exactly like in the sequential reference version).\nstatic int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                                    const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                    const Grid& grid,\n                                    const double threshold,\n                                    Workspace& ws) {\n    const double thr2 = threshold * threshold;\n    const Point sp = points[seed_point];\n\n    ws.members.clear();\n    ws.members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    // Collect all candidates within the threshold of the seed\n    ws.order.clear();\n    const int cx = grid.cellX(sp.x);\n    const int cy = grid.cellY(sp.y);\n    const int x0 = std::max(cx - 1, 0), x1 = std::min(cx + 1, grid.nx - 1);\n    const int y0 = std::max(cy - 1, 0), y1 = std::min(cy + 1, grid.ny - 1);\n    for (int gy = y0; gy <= y1; ++gy) {\n        const int row = gy * grid.nx;\n        for (int gx = x0; gx <= x1; ++gx) {\n            const int b = grid.start[row + gx], e = grid.start[row + gx + 1];\n            for (int p = b; p < e; ++p) {\n                if (grid.idx[p] == seed_point) continue;\n                const double dx = grid.x[p] - sp.x;\n                const double dy = grid.y[p] - sp.y;\n                const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n                if (d2 < thr2) ws.order.push_back(p);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // The reference implementation scans candidates in ascending point id and\n    // keeps the first of several equally good ones; reproduce that ordering.\n    std::sort(ws.order.begin(), ws.order.end(),\n              [&grid](int a, int b) { return grid.idx[a] < grid.idx[b]; });\n\n    int n = static_cast<int>(ws.order.size());\n    ws.idx.resize(n);\n    ws.x.resize(n);\n    ws.y.resize(n);\n    ws.d2.resize(n);\n    int best = -1;\n    double best_d2 = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const int p = ws.order[i];\n        ws.idx[i] = grid.idx[p];\n        ws.x[i] = grid.x[p];\n        ws.y[i] = grid.y[p];\n        const double dx = grid.x[p] - sp.x;\n        const double dy = grid.y[p] - sp.y;\n        const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n        ws.d2[i] = d2;\n        if (d2 < best_d2) {\n            best_d2 = d2;\n            best = i;\n        }\n    }\n\n    int* __restrict cidx = ws.idx.data();\n    double* __restrict cx_ = ws.x.data();\n    double* __restrict cy_ = ws.y.data();\n    double* __restrict cd2 = ws.d2.data();\n\n    // Iteratively add the candidate with the smallest cluster diameter\n    while (best >= 0) {\n        const int newMember = cidx[best];\n        const double nx = cx_[best], ny = cy_[best];\n        ws.members.push_back(newMember);\n\n        // Update the per-candidate maximum distance to the cluster, drop the\n        // candidates that exceeded the threshold and pick the next best one.\n        int w = 0;\n        int nextBest = -1;\n        double nextBestD2 = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            if (i == best) continue;\n            const double dx = cx_[i] - nx;\n            const double dy = cy_[i] - ny;\n            const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            const double m = d2 > cd2[i] ? d2 : cd2[i];\n            if (m < thr2) {\n                cidx[w] = cidx[i];\n                cx_[w] = cx_[i];\n                cy_[w] = cy_[i];\n                cd2[w] = m;\n                if (m < nextBestD2) {\n                    nextBestD2 = m;\n                    nextBest = w;\n                }\n                ++w;\n            }\n        }\n        n = w;\n        best = nextBest;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(ws.members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (distributed over MPI ranks)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    Grid grid;\n    Workspace ws;\n    std::vector<int> best_members;\n    best_members.reserve(N);\n    std::vector<int> recv_members(N);\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        buildGrid(grid, points, unclustered_indices, threshold);\n\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        best_members.clear();\n\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed; seeds are spread round-robin\n        // over the ranks, which balances the very different per-seed costs.\n        const int nseeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n        for (int i = rank; i < nseeds; i += nranks) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            const int cardinality =\n                generateCandidateCluster(seed, points, grid, threshold, ws);\n\n            // Better cardinality, or equal cardinality with a smaller seed id\n            // (the reference scans seeds in ascending order and keeps the first)\n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality ||\n                (cardinality == max_cardinality && seed < best_seed)) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_members = ws.members;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Global reduction: maximize cardinality, break ties by smallest seed.\n        // The key packs both criteria so that a single MAXLOC also yields the\n        // rank owning the winning cluster.\n        struct { long key; int rank; } in, out;\n        in.key = (max_cardinality >= 0)\n                     ? static_cast<long>(max_cardinality) * (N + 1L) + (N - best_seed)\n                     : -1L;\n        in.rank = rank;\n        MPI_Allreduce(&in, &out, 1, MPI_LONG_INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (out.key < 0) break; // No more clusters can be formed\n\n        const int winner = out.rank;\n        const int global_card = static_cast<int>(out.key / (N + 1L));\n        const int global_seed = N - static_cast<int>(out.key % (N + 1L));\n        if (global_card <= 0) break;\n\n        int count = global_card;\n        const int* src;\n        if (rank == winner) {\n            src = best_members.data();\n            MPI_Bcast(const_cast<int*>(src), count, MPI_INT, winner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        } else {\n            MPI_Bcast(recv_members.data(), count, MPI_INT, winner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n            src = recv_members.data();\n        }\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = global_seed;\n        cluster.members.assign(src, src + count);\n        for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {\n            clustered[src[i]] = 1;\n        }\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n        unclustered_indices.erase(\n            std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                          [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n            unclustered_indices.end()\n        );\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold Seeds are distributed round-robin over ranks (round-robin rather than blocked, since per-seed cost correlates strongly with local point density).\n- The globally best candidate is selected with a *single* `MPI_Allreduce(MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_LONG_INT)`: the key packs `cardinality * (N+1) + (N - seed)`, so one reduction maximizes cardinality, breaks ties by the smallest seed id (matching the reference's \"first wins\" scan order), and identifies the owning rank for the follow-up `MPI_Bcast` of the member list.\n- All ranks replicate the deterministic point generation and the `clustered` state, so per-iteration communication is only 2 small messages.\n\n**Serial optimizations (all semantics-preserving)**\n- Incremental max-distance per candidate instead of rescanning all members: O(N·k) instead of O(N·k²).\n- Candidates whose max distance reaches the threshold are compacted out (that quantity is monotonically non-decreasing, so they can never return).\n- A uniform grid (cell size = threshold, rebuilt per iteration over only the unclustered points) limits the initial candidate set to the seed's 3×3 cell block — every member must lie within the threshold of the seed.\n- Squared distances throughout (monotone, so all comparisons are equivalent) and SoA candidate arrays.\n\n**Verification** — hashes are bit-identical to the original sequential binary for `-n 1000`, `-n 3000`, `-n 100`, and identical across 1/2/4/7/8/16/32/64 ranks; `-v` passes in all cases.\n\n| ranks (n=20000) | 1 | 2 | 4 | 8 | 16 | 32 | 64 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| time (ms) | 90926 | 47025 | 22798 | 11511 | 5884 | 3252 | 1782 |\n\nThat is 51× on 64 ranks, on top of a ~420× single-rank algorithmic speedup (n=3000: 478090 ms → 1139 ms).\n\nOne pre-existing bug I did *not* change: for `-n` below ~30 the original `generateSyntheticData` loops forever (`group_cnt` truncates to 0).
